Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any stability module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 361. The projective resolution has length at most 872 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 361 + O(1/n^872). Summing over all modes and applying the stability convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on stability structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 701. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 98 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on stability structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 701. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 98. QED.
